| 2 | **`state` ANY other value** (omitted, `null`, `''`, `'closed '`, `'OPEN'`, a number, a boolean, a `Symbol`, a `12n`, an object, an array, a function, a hostile `Proxy`, a revoked `Proxy`) | **the state is normalized to `'closed'`, and the drive is a NORMALIZE-AND-NOTHING-MOVES drive: `{state: 'closed', changed: false}` for EVERY verb body, `'open'` INCLUDED** — so `('bogus','open')` ⇒ `'closed'`, `changed: false`; `('bogus','toggle')` ⇒ `'closed'`, `changed: false`; `('bogus','close'/'escape'/'unknown'/'dismiss')` ⇒ `'closed'`, `changed: false`. **THE ONE HALF THAT STILL FIRES: a callable `callback` is invoked EXACTLY ONCE on the `'escape'` drive — from an out-of-body state exactly as from `'closed'` — and `0` times on every other verb, with the record above returned either way.** **`String()`/`toString`/`valueOf` invocation counts are `0`** — **⟨CORRECTED 2026-09-28 (finding `R1`; the ruling, its evidence and the drive-by-drive table are at `§0B` item 1) — annotate-beside, nothing deleted: AS FILED this cell read *"the state is normalized to `'closed'`, and every verb acts from there — so `('bogus','open')` ⇒ `'open'`, `changed: true`; …"*, and THAT SENTENCE AND ITS FIRST SUB-ROW ARE WRONG: the verb is never consulted for an out-of-body state, so the `('bogus','open')` ⇒ `'open'`, `changed: true` sub-row is CORRECTED here and is NOT kept as an alternative reading. The as-filed wording is kept visible in this note; the correcting evidence is the vendored mechanism's own `next = isStateBody(state) ? nextStateOf(previous, verb) : previous` (VERIFIED-BY-READ, `src/shared/overlay.ts` → `overlayTransition`), the foundation's `§5.5.1` `P-OV-IM-1` (*"an unusable or out-of-set state reads the declared no-move behaviour from `'closed'` and never a fifth body"*) with its `§2.3` item 2 invariant and `§3.1` `M-3`, and the dossier's §1 rows 3/7.⟩** **⟨RULED 2026-09-29 (Part B (ii); the disposal is at `§0C` item 7 (ii)) — annotate-beside: the SHAPE-LIST entry *"omitted"* names THE ARGUMENT OMITTED, and it is THE SAME VALUE AS AN EXPLICIT `undefined` to the mechanism** (`isStateBody(undefined) === false` on either route), so **this cell's one entry *"omitted"* carries BOTH spellings — *"`undefined` (the argument omitted or passed explicitly)"***. **It is one shape either way, and NO term moves for it.**⟩** | **no** |
| 3 | `verb` one of the five bodies | per the normalization table (`§2.3` item 2) | **no** |
| 4 | **`verb` an unrecognized string or any other shape** | **the declared no-move body; the returned `state` is the caller's own normalized state; `changed: false`; no default verb is applied; an unrecognized string is NOT prefix-matched, folded or trimmed** | **no** |
| 5 | `callback` **absent / `undefined`** | **the declared `{state, changed}` pair is returned and nothing throws** | **no** |
| 6 | `callback` **present but NON-CALLABLE** (`null`, a number, a string, an object, a revoked `Proxy`) | **the same declared pair is returned and nothing throws — an INVOCATION IS ATTEMPTED and its `TypeError` IS ABSORBED; unobservable to the caller.** *"a row asserting that the mechanism refuses an unusable callback is citing a clause this contract does not contain"* (`../Provident-Electron/docs/guide/overlay.md`) — **⟨CORRECTED 2026-09-29 (finding `A-3`; the ruling and its evidence are at `§0C` item 2) — annotate-beside, as-filed wording kept: this cell AS FILED read *"the same declared pair is returned and nothing throws — **no call is attempted**"*, and THE PROSE WAS WRONG, NOT THE IMPLEMENTER'S READING. The vendored bytes guard the callback BY VERB ONLY** (`if (verb === VERB_ESCAPE) { try { (callback as () => void)() } catch { … } }`, VERIFIED-BY-READ of `src/shared/overlay.ts` → `overlayTransition`), **so a non-callable value IS invoked, the engine raises `TypeError`, and the `catch` absorbs it**. **THE OBSERVABLE CLAUSE IS UNCHANGED** — nothing throws, the declared pair is returned, never retried, never retained — **so NO code change and NO test change follows (every landed assertion at this row is a *"does not throw"* assertion), and NO term moves.**⟩ | **no** |
| 7 | `callback` **callable and THROWING**, with a normalized `'escape'` verb | **ONE attempted invocation; the throw is ABSORBED; the declared record is returned; NEVER retried; nothing escapes the call** | **no** |
| 8 | `callback` callable, with **any other** verb (including the no-move body) | **invocation count `0`** — **including on an OUT-OF-BODY `state` with any non-`'escape'` verb** (the guard is on the VERB alone) | **no** |
| 9 | `callback` callable with `'escape'`, **from every one of the four states, `'closed'` included** | **invocation count exactly `1`**; the pair is the matrix's `'escape'` column | **no** |
| **9b** | **`callback` callable with `'escape'`, from an OUT-OF-BODY `state` — THE FIFTH DRIVE GROUP (Part B (i); the disposal is at `§0C` item 7 (i))** | **invocation count exactly `1`** — **the guard is on the VERB alone, so an out-of-body state changes NOTHING about the schedule** — **while the record is the NORMALIZE-AND-NOTHING-MOVES answer `{state: 'closed', changed: false}`** (`§0B` item 1's drive **(4)**). **A fifth ROW id is minted rather than renumbering `10`…`13`**: the four named states are the four adopted **BODIES**, the out-of-body arm is a **separate drive group**, and **no existing row id moves** | **no** |
| 10 | **the callback is NOT RETAINED** — a second call with the same callback observes a fresh count of `1` | no retention, no memo, no history | **no** |
| 11 | **Each call** returns a **fresh, plain, unfrozen** record whose prototype is `Object.prototype`, carrying **exactly two members in declared order** — `Object.keys` reads `['state','changed']`; member values are carried **by identity** | no third member, no getter member, no fifth state body | **no** |
| 12 | **Any invocation with ANY argument shape** | a declared record | **NEVER** — *"NEITHER FUNCTION EVER THROWS, FOR ANY ARGUMENT, AND NEITHER HAS A REFUSAL DOMAIN"* (`overlay.md` §2.1 item 2); **there is no `ok`, `code`, `reason` or `thrown` anywhere** |
| 13 | **THE `G-8` DEFECT — A TRAP, NOT A BEHAVIOUR (R-6).** The declaration's printed identity `removal === (value !== true)` is **unsatisfiable as a strict equation on the set arm** (its `value` is the **string** `'true'`). **This unit's register and drives assert the DECLARED PAIR — `{value: 'true', removal: false}` on the set arm and `{value: false, removal: true}` on every other arm — and NEVER the printed equation.** **A pass that "fixes" the foundation's text or the vendored bytes to satisfy the equation is committing the two forbidden acts** (`R-14`; `R-4`). **Its row exists: `docs/defects.md` → `docs/HANDOFF.md` `G-8 OVERLAY-REMOVAL-IDENTITY-UNSATISFIABLE-AS-PRINTED`.** |

### 3.2 The adapter's own fail-states — `createModalController` (TOTAL — the CONTRACT this unit owns)

| # | Shape | Declared outcome |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `options` **omitted** | a working controller, `isOpen() === false` |
| 2 | `options` **`null`**, a number, a string, a `Symbol`, a function, a `12n` | total: a working controller, `isOpen() === false`, **no throw** |
| 3 | `options` **an object with a non-boolean `initialOpen`** (`'yes'`, `1`, `0`, `null`, `{}`, `[]`, `NaN`) | **coerced to `false`** (strict `=== true` is the only true form); `isOpen() === false` |
| 4 | `options` **an object with `initialOpen: true`** | `isOpen() === true` at construction; **no callback fires at construction** |
| 5 | `options` with **non-function `onOpen`/`onClose`/`onToggle`** | each is a **no-op**; never invoked; **no throw** |
| 6 | `options` with a **throwing** `onOpen`/`onClose`/`onToggle` | **the throw is absorbed; the transition still completes; the controller never throws** |
| 7 | `options` **a frozen object**, or one whose known keys are **accessors**, or a **revoked `Proxy`** | **`[H]`-class; UNVERIFIED by this pass.** The landed implementation reads the four keys by plain property access, so a **throwing getter at a known key would throw** — recorded as an INFO-class limit at `docs/specs/unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.md` §3c (`SH7-ADV4`) and **carried unchanged**. **What would settle it:** the unit's own red run. **This spec does NOT widen the totality claim to cover it** ⟨**SETTLED 2026-09-28 BY THE GATE-5 BLIND RUN AND RULED BY THE SUPERVISOR — annotate-beside, the as-filed item is KEPT and now AGREES with `§2.1`.** **WHAT SETTLED IT (the greens artifact's row `A9`, measurer: that pass's runner):** a **frozen options object does NOT throw**; **a THROWING accessor at one of the four known keys DOES throw**; **a revoked `Proxy` DOES throw** (`TypeError`) — exactly the reading this item predicted. **THE RULING: THIS ITEM IS THE CONTRACT.** `§2.1`'s totality cell was **corrected to this reading** (it had named the frozen object, the hostile getter and the revoked `Proxy` as total in the same file that declines to widen the claim), so **the two cells now agree and the `A9` `FAIL` is disposed as SPEC STALENESS, not as a behaviour gap.** **THE RESIDUAL IS CARRIED AS A MINTED DEFECT ROW, NOT SMOOTHED:** the four option reads are not hardened under the module's own fail-soft discipline, and **no landed register row covers the throwing-accessor or revoked-`Proxy` shapes** (`§4` `P-MD-IM-1`'s malformed-option list does not name them) — filed as **`MODAL-OPTION-READ-NOT-FAIL-SOFT`** in `docs/defects.md`, **owner: the next pass that owns `src/renderer/modal-state.ts`; no code change in this pass.**⟩** |
| 8 | **Every method** — `open()`/`close()`/`toggle()`/`isOpen()` on ANY controller built from ANY of the above | **never throws** |
| 9 | **Repeated calls**: `open()` on open · `close()` on closed | **idempotent no-ops** — the state does not move, **`changed` is `false`**, the corresponding notification **does not fire**, and **the class mirror performs no write** |
| 10 | **`toggle()`** | **always a move from both reachable bodies** (`'closed'`→`'open'`, `'open'`→`'closed'`); **never a no-op**; fires the direction's notification **then** `onToggle` |
| 11 | **`isOpen()` with no transition between reads** | **stable for 100 consecutive reads** — free of side effects |
| 12 | **Two controllers built from deep-equal options and driven by identical sequences** | **identical `isOpen()` traces** — no module-level state, no memo, no cache |
| 13 | **The adapter's file** | **no DOM read of any kind besides the wiring's own `document` guard and its four `getElementById` calls plus the `appendChild` calls**; **no `matchMedia`**; **no ambient `document`/`window` read inside `createModalController`**; **no listener inside the controller** |

### 3.3 The seam (`callback`) — ALL THREE ARMS, AND THE INVOCATION SCHEDULE

| Arm | The `callback` value | Declared outcome |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **(a) ABSENT / `undefined`** | the option omitted, or `undefined` | the declared `{state, changed}` pair is returned; **nothing is attempted; nothing throws** |
| **(b) NON-CALLABLE** | `null`, a number, a string, an object, an array, a revoked `Proxy` | the same declared pair is returned; **an invocation IS ATTEMPTED and its `TypeError` is ABSORBED — unobservable to the caller** — **⟨CORRECTED 2026-09-29 (finding `A-3`; the ruling and its evidence are at `§0C` item 2) — annotate-beside, as-filed title/arm kept: this arm AS FILED read *"**NON-CALLABLE** … **no call attempted**; nothing throws"*. THE PROSE WAS WRONG, NOT THE IMPLEMENTER'S READING: the vendored guard is on the VERB ALONE** (`if (verb === VERB_ESCAPE) { try { callback() } catch {} }`, VERIFIED-BY-READ of `src/shared/overlay.ts`), **so a non-callable value is invoked, a `TypeError` is raised and absorbed**. **The observable clause is UNCHANGED** — nothing throws, the declared pair is returned — **so no code change, no test change and no term move follows.**⟩ |
| **(c) THROWING** | a function that throws | **ONE attempted invocation, the throw ABSORBED, the declared record returned, NEVER retried** |

**The invocation schedule, pinned exactly:** **exactly once · only when the normalized verb is `'escape'` · for
every one of the four states, `'closed'` included · never on `'open'`/`'close'`/`'toggle'`/the no-move body ·
never retained between calls · a throw never propagates out of `close()`.** **⟨AMENDED 2026-09-29 (Part B (i); the
disposal is at `§0C` item 7 (i)) — annotate-beside, as-filed sentence kept: THE SCHEDULE CARRIES A FIFTH DRIVE GROUP
— **FROM AN OUT-OF-BODY `state`**, the verb-only guard fires the callback **exactly once** on `'escape'` and **`0`**
times on every other verb (`§3.1` item **9b**; `§0B` item 1's drive **(4)**), with the record still
`{state: 'closed', changed: false}`. **The four named states are the four adopted BODIES; the out-of-body arm is a
separate drive GROUP, not a fifth body and not a fifth STATE** — so **`§3.1` items 8/9/10's four-state arm is
UNMOVED, and no register term moves** (the register's `P-MD-IM-2` *"four states"* is a **body** count and stays
`4`). This is what makes `§3.1` item 2's corrected cell and this schedule clause consistent.⟩** **What the fork hands in:** **`options.callback`,
which the wiring does not set** — so on every landed route the argument is absent, and the absence is the
foundation's own declared arm (`§2.5` clause 3).

### 3.4 The adapter's fail-states — `installSettingsModal` (FAIL-SOFT + IDEMPOTENT)

| # | Shape | Declared outcome |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | **No DOM** (`typeof document === 'undefined'`, or `document.getElementById` missing/not a function) | **no-op, no throw** |
| 2 | **Any one of the four elements absent** (frame / toggle / scrim / body) | **no-op, no throw** — no listeners, no re-parent, no class write |
| 3 | **`#app` / app-graph panes / `#tab-strip` / the gutters present but the operator mounts absent** | **no re-parent of anything else; never throws** |
| 4 | **A DOUBLE install on the same document** | **a no-op** — exactly ONE toggle listener, ONE document keydown, ONE scrim listener, ONE re-parent |
| 5 | **An install on a DIFFERENT document** (a fresh shim per test) | wires independently — the marker is per-document |
| 6 | **The body lacks `appendChild`** | that mount is skipped; **no throw** |
| 7 | **A frozen or read-only frame `className`** | the write fails and is **absorbed**; **no throw**; behaviour otherwise unchanged (**`[H]`-class; UNVERIFIED — what would settle it: the unit's red run**) |
| 8 | **A click on `#settings-toggle` before the frame exists** | **no throw**; nothing happens |
| 9 | **A document `keydown` with a non-`Escape` key** (`'Enter'`, `'KeyE'`, `' '`, `'Scape'`, `''`, `'Escape '`, `'KeyEscape'`) | **ignored** — no close, **no throw**, no class write. **The comparison is the literal `e.key === 'Escape'`; no trim, no fold** |
| 10 | **A click on a nested content child of `#settings-modal-body`** | **not** a scrim click — the modal stays open |
| 11 | **A scrim click while closed**, and **an Escape while closed** | idempotent no-ops: `changed: false` ⇒ **no class write**, no notification (`§2.5` clause 4) |
| 12 | **The marker committed then a retry** | `SH7-ADV6`-class **INFO** limit, carried unchanged: the marker is set at the end, and a pre-commit throw is unreachable today (the re-parent `appendChild` is guarded). **Not widened by this unit** |
| 13 | **Alternating documents A→B→A** | `SH7-ADV5`-class **INFO** limit, carried unchanged: one marker tracks one document. **Unreachable in production** (one document per process) |
| 14 | **Any of the above** | **never breaks boot, never throws** — the whole body is inside one `try`/`catch` |

### 3.5 The seams (there are NONE outside `callback` — `R-3`, restated as a checkable claim)

**`U-OVERLAY` declares an EMPTY seam set.** What the fork supplies is **data and the wiring's own work**:

| The fork supplies | Where |
| --- | --- |
| the **`state` word** — its whole vocabulary and its reachable subset | the adapter (`§2.3` items 1/5) — **fork-side, never pushed into the mechanism as a decision** |
| the **`verb` word**, from the four-body subset the adapter emits | the adapter's four methods (`§2.3` item 4) |
| the **`callback`**, as an argument (a declared no-op on every landed route) | `ModalControllerOptions.callback`, unset by the wiring (`§2.5`) |
| the **frame, the toggle, the scrim, the body, the two mounts, the class vocabulary, the CSS** | `src/renderer/index.html` + the wiring (**unchanged**, `§1.2` (b)/(c)) |
| **the re-parent itself** | the wiring's two `appendChild` calls (**RETained against the foundation's refusal**, `§2.7`) |
| **the applied inert write** | **NOBODY in this unit** — the consumer is the parked row `PD-OVERLAY-2` (`§1.2` (d)) |

## 4. The typed Property register (`§5.x`-class; **CODE-BEARING UNIT — the register is REQUIRED**)

**Section-number note (so a citation resolves):** the house convention calls this block a **`§5.x` Property
register**; **in THIS file it is `§4`**, because `§3` is this unit's mechanics block — the same placement the
sibling unit used (`docs/specs/unit-pd-ui-1-theme.md` §4).

**Row-id discipline (`C-5`, and it is binding):** every row is **`P-MD-*`** — never a bare `P-IM-`/`P-SM-`/`P-TP-`
id, because **the fork's target file already carries those bare ids with different meanings**
(`P-IM-1` · `P-SM-1` · `P-SM-2` · `P-TP-1` · `P-TP-2` · `P-TP-3` in
`tests/unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.test.ts`) and **the foundation carries `P-OV-*`** in
`../Provident-Electron/docs/specs/overlay.md` §5.5.1. **Strategy ids are `strat:pd-ui-6-*`** — never the fork's
`strat:modal-*` and never the foundation's `S-OV-*`. **No `F-` row and no `§6`/`FS-n` citation appears in this
register.**

**Why a register and not the zero-row exemption.** This unit **is code-bearing** (it changes two `src/**` modules),
and its central claims are **quantifications over finite shape sets** (the malformed-option shapes, the state
shapes, the `4 × 5` cells, the class-write shapes). `PBT-REGISTER-REQUIRED-FOR-CODE-UNITS` **forbids the zero-row
exemption for a code-bearing unit**, and **no superseded `§5.5.0` exemption exists in this repo** — so the register
is authored, not exempted.

**Shared machinery (pinned once, binding on every row).**

- **Seed:** **`0x55E11E77`** — **the fork's "SHELL"-family seed, REUSED, and its reuse is DECLARED here rather than
  assumed.** The constant and its `mulberry32` generator are **pinned in the target test file**
  (`tests/unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.test.ts`), which this unit archives (`§6.3`); **re-deriving the harness in
  the unit's new file with the SAME literal keeps one seed for the SHELL family and avoids minting a second
  generator family in the same subject area.** **Never** `Date.now()`, never `Math.random()`, never an environment
  read. **The generator is used ONLY by the rows whose strategy declares a drawn domain; a row with a closed
  hand-picked enumeration declares that instead and runs no draw.**
- **Caps:** **≤100 attempts per row, ≤400 in total**, the terms printed as their sums; rows run **sequentially in
  register order**; **STOP AFTER 5 CONSECUTIVE FAILURES** (the running row's remaining attempts are abandoned; no
  further row starts). **No new dependency, no PBT library, no sixth leg** — plain deterministic vitest tables in
  the unit's own test file(s).
- **Terms (`REGISTER-ATTEMPT-TOTALS-PRINT-THEIR-TERMS`):** every term is printed **as the sum of its own
  factors**; a term that counts **distinct inputs** rather than **drives** says so.
- **Control-draw reporting (required):** every row carries a **control whose expected outcome is the OPPOSITE of
  the row's verdict**, and the row is `held` **only if the control discriminates**. **The report prints `held` /
  `broken` per row with the strategy id.**
- **THE CONTROL RULE, RESTATED AS A CHECKABLE REQUIREMENT (2026-09-29, finding `A-2`; the disposal is at `§0C`
  item 1).** **The as-filed sentence above is KEPT and is now BINDING IN THIS EXACT FORM:** **(1)** a control **MUST
  BE ABLE TO FAIL for a corpus that SATISFIES the row** — i.e. it must be a **falsifier of the row's own verdict**,
  not a restatement of a mechanism cell the row already asserts; **(2)** a control's **SUBJECT MUST BE THE ROW'S OWN
  INSTRUMENT** (the adapter corpus, the row's domain, the scan/oracle the row uses) — a control driven against an
  **unrelated object**, a **hand-invoked callback**, or **an `any`-shaped disjunction** does **not** qualify; **(3)**
  a control whose expected outcome is **structurally unreachable** (an oracle that cannot be driven, an inverted
  predicate, a tautology) is **`broken`**, and the row is **not `held`**, exactly as a failed drive. **THE SIX
  NON-FALSIFYING CONTROLS `A-2` NAMED ARE THE FIRST APPLICATIONS OF THIS RULE, each owed a re-state by the
  TestWriter (`NG-SM4-CONTROLS` + the row-specific ids):** **`P-MD-IM-1` CONTROL 3** (drives an *unrelated object's*
  `toString`) · **`P-MD-IM-2` CONTROLS 3/5** (call the callback *by hand*) · **`P-MD-SM-1` CONTROLS 2/4** (restate
  the *mechanism's own cells*) · **`P-MD-TP-1` CONTROLS 2/3** (restate the *mechanism*) · **`P-MD-SM-3` CONTROL 3**
  (reduces to a *tautology*) · **`P-MD-SM-4` CONTROL 1** (an *any-config-passes disjunction*). **Their row's
  declared control COUNT is unchanged (a control is a term, and no term moves for a control's re-state); what
  changes is that each must now be shown to DISCRIMINATE.**
- **ADAPTER-ROUTE REQUIREMENT (2026-09-29, finding `A-1` (i)/(ii)/(iii); the disposal is at `§0C` item 1).** **A row
  whose property text claims that a value is *"passed through `ModalControllerOptions.callback`"*, that the
  *"adapter emits"* a verb or state, that the *"adapter fabricates none"*, or that the *"class mirror"* writes or
  does not write, **MUST drive the ADAPTER** — the register's **recording-stub route** (the adapter corpus
  instantiated with a recording/injected transition) — and **MUST NOT satisfy the claim by calling the vendored
  `overlayTransition` directly**. **A direct mechanism call may be used for a claim whose text says it is about the
  MECHANISM (the matrix rows), and the text must say which it is.** **The rows this rule binds by name:
  `P-MD-IM-1`, `P-MD-IM-2`, `P-MD-SM-2`, `P-MD-TP-2`** (the four whose as-filed texts over-claimed the adapter
  route — `A-1`, `A-11`, `A-12`).
- **⟨RECORDED 2026-09-28 BY THE ITEM-10d DOCUMENTATION REVIEW — THE `X-2` GAP, WITH ITS TWO ADMISSIBLE RESOLUTIONS
  AND ITS OWNER (annotate-beside, `RCA-8(c)`; the requirement above is KEPT verbatim and is NOT relaxed).⟩** **THE GAP,
  VERIFIED-BY-READ:** this requirement names *"the register's recording-stub route"*, but **the documented surface is
  `createModalController(options?: ModalControllerOptions)` with four declared option members and NO injection seam**
  (`§2.1`) — while **`§6.4` item 1 forecloses the obvious module-mock route** (`vi.mock`/`vi.spyOn`/`vi.hoisted` in
  any form). **CONSEQUENCE, as the gate-5 blind pass measured it: the two register rows this requirement binds —
  `P-MD-IM-2` (ii) and `P-MD-TP-2` (6b) (a)/(c)/(d) — could NOT be turned into a runnable expectation from the
  documentation alone** (the greens artifact's rows `B10`/`C7` are `NOT-BLIND-DERIVABLE`), **and the FALSE-GREEN of
  `§0C` item 6 (an `isOpen()`-recomputing implementation passing every landed row) is not closable from the
  documented surface either.** **WHAT THE LANDED FILE ACTUALLY DOES, VERIFIED-BY-READ so no later pass re-derives it:
  the route is a SUBSTITUTED-IMPORT INSTRUMENT** — `tests/pd-ui-6-modal-state-register.test.ts` compiles the
  adapter's OWN source through `new Function(…)` while substituting the vendored import binding
  (`__PD_UI_6_TRANSITION__`) with a recording/injected transition, so the drives reach the ADAPTER (never the
  mechanism directly) **without binding the vitest mock API in any form.** **It is a real route, and the contract
  does not name it — that is the finding.** **THE TWO ADMISSIBLE RESOLUTIONS (both doc-side; NEITHER is taken here):
  (a) document the substituted-import instrument as part of this unit's DOCUMENTED SURFACE** (what is substituted,
  at which seam, and why it is not a mock binding); **or (b) restate the bound rows as `[T]`-UNCLOSABLE and accept
  the weaker claim, recording that `§0C` item 6's false-green stays open until (a) is taken.** **OWNER: the spec pass
  that owns `docs/specs/unit-pd-ui-6-modal-state.md` (the supervisor assigns it); a documentation review may not
  author the missing derivation.** **NO register term moves under either resolution, and this pass writes no test
  byte.**
- **Class meaning here:** **`P-MD-IM` = an invariant of the ADOPTED contract (the mechanism's declared domain, arms
  and records) as the fork consumes it** · **`P-MD-SM` = a safety property over the repo/app state this unit may
  not disturb** · **`P-MD-TP` = a totality / discrimination property over this unit's own adapter surface.**
- **`(bounded)`** is marked where a row's property text quantifies more broadly than the finite domain it drives —
  **a bounded row is execution design, not a proof of the universal it states.**
- **Layer:** every `[T]`-class row is node/pure or a source-text read; **no row is app-green**, and no row may be
  reported as app evidence (`RCA-12`).
- **THE DECLARED PAIR, NEVER THE PRINTED EQUATION (`R-6`):** any row touching the declaration asserts
  `{value: 'true', removal: false}` / `{value: false, removal: true}` — **never `removal === (value !== true)`**.

## 5. The layer ledger — what this unit does NOT claim

1. **No app-green, no live-green, no envelope-green-as-app.** No claim in this file is evidence that the Electron
   app boots, paints the modal, hit-tests the scrim, or reacts to a real `Escape` (`RCA-12`; `G-6`).
2. **No `divergence` green.** The leg is RED at this head for an **environmental** reason and is **mandatory
   pre-live** (`R-10`) — a precondition this unit **records and passes through**, never satisfies. **⟨AMENDED
   2026-09-29 (finding `A-13`; the disposition is at `§0C` item 1) — annotate-beside, the as-filed layer claim is
   kept and UNCHANGED: THE UNIT'S LIVE-LAYER STATE IS `OPEN / live-pending` — NEVER A SILENT PARK** (`RCA-11`), **and
   the four `[U]` claims the live rows would cover are named `UNTAKEN`: (i) a real click opening the modal; (ii) a
   real `Escape` closing it; (iii) a real scrim click closing it; (iv) the re-parented mounts painting** (`§7.2`).
   **A node-green is envelope-green only** (`RCA-12`), and **the DONE row must carry the reading, the
   `PRECONDITION-FAILED` status and the named owner (`U-DIVERGENCE-SPAWN`).**⟩**
3. **No `battery` claim beyond the recorded reading** — the branch baseline reads **`184 checks, 0 failures`**
   (GREEN) at the program's baseline, and that is a **HARNESS** reading (`[H]`), **never app-green**.
   **`G-5` obliges every UI unit's trio to GAIN `npm run battery`** — this unit runs it and reports its own reading
   (`§7.1`).
4. **No `conformance`-leg claim of this unit's own** — and **no claim that the vendored `overlay.ts` is covered by
   it**: all fifteen vendored modules carry **ZERO behaviour evidence** from that leg
   (`docs/specs/unit-pd-vendor-foundation-mechanisms.md` §13.2 `C4`: class (i) is EMPTY; the leg's pass condition is
   **VACUOUS AS EVIDENCE**). **`PD-UI-1` and `PD-UI-6` are the first units whose rows drive a vendored module at
   all — and that is a strengthening of this repo's coverage, not an inheritance of someone else's.**
5. **No claim that the foundation's `U-OVERLAY` green is app evidence** — inherited **CONTRACT** evidence only
   (`§0`'s inherited-evidence rule).
6. **No claim about the modal's VISIBILITY.** The node-testable proxy for "hidden" is the **class-token XOR**
   (`docs/specs/unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.md` §2.3); the `display: none` outcome and `getComputedStyle` are
   **browser-only and NOT node-asserted**.
7. **No claim about hit-testing.** The scrim's `pointer-events: auto` is a **`[T]`/static** reading of
   `src/renderer/index.html`; that a real click LANDS on the scrim is a `[U]` claim (`SH7-ADV1`'s own recorded
   reason: *"the dom-shim has NO hit-testing"*).
8. **No claim about the applied inert write, the focus trap, or the top layer** — the first is the parked
   `PD-OVERLAY-2`; the other two stay **OPEN GAPS** (`P-OV-11`; `docs/specs/ui-overhaul.md` §2.1's Limits).
9. **No store, no persistence, no MCP-surface claim** — this unit adds none and changes none. **The modal's open
   state stays session-only transient shell state** (`W-U-SHELL-7-Q2`, unchanged).
10. **No claim that this unit's node green covers the `renderer.ts` call-site edit's runtime effect** — that edit is
    **`[H]`** (one line, source-text-visible) and nothing more.

| **4** | **`P-MD-TP-2`** — verb discipline | **red without a rendered surface**: the adapter emits no verbs at all at this head, so the row fails on its first drive, naming the method. |

| **6** | **`P-MD-SM-1`, `P-MD-SM-3`, `P-MD-SM-4`** — the class mirror, the vendored-and-kept-half, and pin safety | **red without a rendered surface**: at the red head the class mirror is green (the landed `settle` guard) **but its guard is not the adopted one**, so `P-MD-SM-1`'s counted-write limb and `P-MD-SM-3`'s import-census limb red (the adapter's vendored import reads `0` and must read `1`); **and `P-MD-SM-4` (e) reds the moment the adapter lands** — that is the **escalated, expected collision** of `§3.6.1`, which the red run must REPORT as a distinct red file. |

### 6.2 What the red is NOT

Not a `src/shared/**` byte change (no byte of the vendored set, and none of the four baseline files) · **not a
sibling unit's cycle** (`PD-UI-1` is W1's other row and **shares no cycle** — `RCA-2`/`RCA-5`) · not a fence edit ·
not a `vitest.config.ts` or `package.json` edit · not a tracker rewrite · **not a repoint of any citation on the
archived file's own pass** · not an edit of **any** existing test file other than the archive move itself
(`§1.4`) · **not a `vi.mock('electron', …)` or any other vitest-mock binding** (`§6.4`) · **not a new live matrix
slot** (`§7.2`) · **not an `inert` write, an attribute name, a route or a background identity** (`§1.2` (d)).

### 6.3 THE TEST-DISPOSITION QUESTION, ANSWERED FOR ALL 40 ROWS (`DECIDED: REBUILD-ARCHIVE-POLICY`)

**The governing rule, quoted in its own terms (clause (1)):** *"When the specs supersede a test's subject, the file
is moved out of `tests/**` into `archive/tests/<date>-<name>.test.ts` — it is **never** edited, relaxed, skipped,
`todo`'d or rewritten in place to keep the suite green."* **(clause (2)):** *"The behaviours an archived file pinned
are re-derived from the SPEC by the owning unit's own gated pass … with a FRESH red set … The archived file is
consulted as an input to what the old pin covered, never as the source of the new contract, and its assertions are
never copied back."* **(clause (3)):** *"Every retirement records its file count / row count / skip count before →
after as a READING from `npm test` (never a prediction) …"*

**THE RULING: THE 40-ROW FILE IS ARCHIVED WHOLE — moved verbatim, never edited — AND EVERY CLASS'S BEHAVIOUR IS
RE-DERIVED BY THIS UNIT'S GATED CYCLE IN NEW FILES.**

**The row-class table — all 40 rows disposed of, per class, with the reason:**

| **2** | **the 2 re-parent rows** | `state 12` (incl. its `modalBody.children.length === 2` HARD-INVARIANT assertion), `F10` | **ARCHIVED with the file; RE-DERIVED UNCHANGED IN SUBSTANCE — the unit's REGRESSION GUARD** | the behaviour is **KEPT** (`§2.7`; the foundation **refuses** the half, `R-2`). **The recorded `SPLIT` reason's *"the re-parent rows survive"* is honoured in SUBSTANCE — survive as re-derived rows — not by leaving the file in place** (`§6.3` item 1) |
| **3** | **the 18 wiring rows** (7 `§3b` + 9 `§4` dom-shim — of which 2 are class 2's) | `state 8`..`state 14` (minus `state 12`), `F3`..`F12` (minus `F10`) | **ARCHIVED with the file; RE-DERIVED. `state 8`..`state 11`/`state 13`/`state 14`, `F3`..`F8`, `F11`, `F12` are BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVED rows** — they are re-derived **because every wiring row's call shape changes** with the dropped parameters (`§2.6`) and because their class-write guard moves to `changed` (`§2.4`); the re-derived rows are the **regression guard** | **these 18 rows are among the 24 the recorded `SPLIT` reason left UNCLASSIFIED**, and this ruling is where they are classified. **They are not superseded in BEHAVIOUR but they are superseded as CALL SHAPES** — `REBUILD-ARCHIVE-POLICY` clause (2) forbids adapting them in place |

| **5** | **the 3 adversarial regressions** | `SH7-ADV2` (the counted class writes / `settle` no-op guard), `SH7-ADV3` (sibling-class survival), `SH7-ADV1` (the `index.html` scrim `pointer-events: auto` source pin — **the file's only `M-html` row**) | **ARCHIVED with the file; RE-DERIVED, ALL THREE, UNCHANGED IN SUBSTANCE** | `SH7-ADV2` is **the tripwire this unit's Escape ruling depends on** (`§2.5`'s pin table); `SH7-ADV3` pins a class-mirror property `§2.4` keeps; `SH7-ADV1` reads a file **this unit does not touch**, and its assertion must be re-derived **verbatim** so the ADV1 fix keeps a guard. **None may be dropped** |
| **6** | **the 1 `M-html` row** | `SH7-ADV1` (also counted in class 5 — **it is the same single row, named in both places on purpose: nothing else in this file reads `index.html`**) | **RE-DERIVED — verbatim assertion** | it is the **only** `M-html` reader (`§1.1` item 6), and its subject (`src/renderer/index.html`'s scrim rule) is **DENIED to this unit** (`§1.4`) — so the re-derived row must assert **exactly** what the archived row asserted, so the archive's coverage hole is **zero for this row** |

**The five clauses that make this ruling complete:**

1. **The whole file moves; no row is left at the old path.** `tests/unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.test.ts` is this

   (`§1.1` item 2), and **every one of its seven classes is touched by this unit** (the call shape, the guard, the
   verb binding, the source pins). **Leaving it in place would keep 40 rows pinning superseded or re-shaped
   subjects** — and clause (1) forbids editing it in place. **Therefore the archive is the whole file, and the

   is landed: the file's current address is `archive/tests/2026-09-28-unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.test.ts` and **NO
   file remains at the old `tests/**` path** (a move, never a copy — `§6.4` item 6 satisfied). **EVERY CITATION OF

### 6.4 The `[T]`-side obligations the red set carries by name

1. **The unit's own test file must NOT mock `'electron'`** and **must not bind the vitest mock API in any form** —
   `tests/pd-vendor-set.test.ts`'s `A-12` row pins the electron census at exactly five names and **names** the four
   non-`'electron'` binders (`§1.1` item 13); **a sixth binding of any form reds it.**
2. **It must not read anything a `G-9` pin freezes** (`§3.6` items 3/5).
3. **Its `describe`/`it` titles must be census-stable** — one row per declared property and one per declared term,
   so a term dropped from the table without a contract amendment is a **loud failure**, never a vacuous pass.
4. **The register's terms must be printed as their sums and the total printed with them** (`§4`).
5. **The re-derived live rows must be authored against `scripts/live-drive.mjs`'s existing blocks only** — see
   `§7.2`; **a new block or a new `MATRIX_ROWS` slot reds `tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts`.**
6. **The archived file must be moved, not copied** — a copy left at the old path **re-enters the suite** and reds it
   for the wrong reason.

### 7.2 THE LIVE MANDATE, THE RE-PIN, AND THE HONEST INTERIM STATE AT THIS HEAD

**This unit renders an interactive overlay, so the pre-DONE live battery is MANDATORY (`RCA-11` clause (a)).** The
claims that only a live/assembled pass can take:

| # | The live claim | Why node cannot take it | Which existing block drives it |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **L-1** | a **REAL click** on `#settings-toggle` opens the modal (the frame's class flips in a real document) | the dom-shim's synthetic dispatch is not a hit-tested gesture, and the boot path is the assembled renderer | `ufModal`'s open path (a real CDP click), inside `uf_layout_10` |
| **L-2** | a **real `Escape` keydown** closes it | requires the real key event + the real document listener | `ufModal`'s close path (`ufKey`) |
| **L-3** | a **real click on the scrim** closes it, and a **content click does not** | requires **hit-testing**, which the dom-shim does not have (`SH7-ADV1`'s own recorded reason) | the modal blocks' scrim/content points |
| **L-4** | the **re-parented mounts are PAINTED** inside the modal body (a non-zero box, populated text) | requires layout | the modal block's `#panes`/`#operator-panes` painted checks |

**THE RE-PIN — THE MECHANISM, AND THE ROW (a RE-PIN of an existing `§5.U` row, NEVER a new slot):**

| Field | Reading |

| **The mechanism — exact** | **Inside `uf_layout_10` (i.e. inside `U-5`'s existing primary block, which is where `U-4`'s primary block also points), add an assertion that the frame carries exactly one of `is-open`/`is-closed` at every state boundary the block already crosses — before the open, inside the open, and after the close — using the class read `ufModal` already performs.** **NO new block · NO new `MATRIX_ROWS` slot · NO row-id change · NO change to `summary.total`.** **`ufModal`'s existing behaviour is the instrument** (`/is-open/` on `#settings-modal`'s `className`; a real click to open; `ufKey('Escape')` to close). **⟨STATUS 2026-09-29 (finding `A-9`; the disposition is at `§0C` item 1, the owed item at `§0C` item 3) — annotate-beside: this row is the SPECIFICATION OF AN OWED ARTIFACT, not a record of one. VERIFIED-BY-READ this pass: `scripts/live-drive.mjs` carries NO such assertion at this head** — the re-pin is **authored but UNEXECUTED**, its owner is **the landing pass's live step**, and until it lands **`L-1`..`L-4` are `UNTAKEN`**.⟩** **⟨UPDATED 2026-09-28 BY THE ITEM-10d DOCUMENTATION REVIEW (`RCA-8(c)`: the `A-9` note above is KEPT as that pass's reading) — THE ARTIFACT IS NOW LANDED; THE LIVE LAYER IS STILL NOT GREEN, FOR A DIFFERENT REASON.⟩** **VERIFIED-BY-READ at this head: `scripts/live-drive.mjs` → `uf_layout_10` CARRIES the class-XOR assertion exactly as this row specifies — one `x(className)` counter, a read before the open, inside the open and after the close, no new block, no new `MATRIX_ROWS` slot (`U-1`…`U-8` unmoved, `uf_layout_10` still the primary block of `U-4`/`U-5`) and no change to `summary.total`; `tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts` carries `30` `it()` blocks.** **CONSEQUENCE, and it is the whole point of not smoothing it: `L-1`…`L-4` remain `UNTAKEN` — NOT because the artifact is missing any more, but because the LIVE LEG itself is `PRECONDITION-FAILED` (the environmental `/dev/shm` denial; owner `U-DIVERGENCE-SPAWN`).** **THE LIVE-LAYER STATE THEREFORE STAYS `OPEN / live-pending`: a node green is envelope-green only, and this unit has taken NO app-layer reading.** |

## 8. Decisions and defaults (recorded, so no later pass re-derives them)

| # | Decision | Default taken |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **D-1** | Where the adapter lives | **the EXISTING `src/renderer/modal-state.ts`** — **no new module, no new file** (the same choice the sibling unit made, for the same reason: a new module would leave the fork's existing state machine in place beside the adopted one, which is the second-authority hazard the adoption exists to avoid) |
| **D-2** | How the vendored module is obtained | **by a static `import`** (`'../shared/overlay.js'`, resolving to `src/shared/overlay.ts`). **Never edited, never copied, never re-vendored, never shadowed by a local duplicate** (`R-4`); **never evaded** by `await import(...)`/`require(...)`/`new URL(...)` (`§3.6.1`) |
| **D-3** | Whether the adapter may re-own or wrap the mechanism's decision | **NO.** The adapter **calls** `overlayTransition` and **reads** its record; it does **not** re-implement the matrix, does **not** cache a decision, and does **not** keep a second state authority (`§2.4`, `§2.3` item 5 clause 2) |
| **D-4** | Whether this unit applies, names or routes the inert write | **NO — EXCLUDED by the architect's ruling** (`§1.2` (d)); the consumer is **declared absent and named** (`PD-OVERLAY-2`, PARKED) |
| **D-5** | Whether `'held'`/`'closing'` may be entered, emulated or forbidden | **ALL THREE ARE `NO`**: the adapter holds the adopted word, never fabricates a body, and **no row requires or forbids the fork's use of them** (`§2.3` item 5) |
| **D-6** | The fate of `tests/unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.test.ts` | **ARCHIVED WHOLE, never edited in place; every class's behaviour re-derived in NEW files with a fresh red set; the before → after reading owed at landing** (`§6.3`). **The sibling unit's contrary `D-3` is contradicted, with reasons** (`§6.3` item 5) |
| **D-7** | Whether `ModalControllerOptions` may gain a member | **YES — exactly ONE, `callback?: unknown`**, and its purpose is `§2.5`'s invocation schedule. **No other member is added, and the four pinned members keep their names, optionality and semantics** |
| **D-8** | Whether a new exported type may be declared | **YES — ONE: `ModalVerb`** (`'open' \| 'close' \| 'toggle' \| 'escape'`), for the reason at `§2.1`. **The four existing exports keep their names; nothing is removed from the module's export census except the `installSettingsModal` parameters** |
